- **derisk-gate**: Before decomposing a plan into units on architecturally risky work: a new package boundary, an unproven integration, a cross-cutting concern, or a load/latency assumption. Prove the riskiest first by wiring a thin runnable path end to end (a spike, or walking skeleton) against the real dependency, then emit a ranked risk register and a GREEN / RED / OVERRIDE verdict. Fits 'is this plan risky', 'prove it works end to end first', 'de-risk this', or asking whether a plan or integration is safe to commit to. Skip explicitly, never silently, for incremental work over a proven architecture. ([AlexTavor/engineering-discipline](https://github.com/AlexTavor/engineering-discipline/tree/HEAD/skills/derisk-gate))
